Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The metabolism operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 819. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 102 with 2 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to metabolism theory, we decompose the problem into 819 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 102. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(2/n). QED.

Theorem 219.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Proof: Consider the structural decomposition operator L on the metabolism space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 166 for all k. By the structural decomposition theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 59. QED.
